published in the Gazette of In lia of the 20th April 1946 the following list is hereby advertised of securitios logt eto , in respect 
of which primz facie grounds exiss for believing that the spirities havo been lost and that the claim of the applicants is just .

im nediately with the Manager, Reserve Bank of India , Calcutta .
